Compile excludes AST construction/Light conversion. Arithmetic construct rows rebuild the shared System AST for both versions; they do not measure a native Light AST builder. Modern compile+first adds first execution of the generated delegate; Framework restricted anonymous hosting can already JIT during compilation, so phase costs are not mechanically interchangeable. Framework arithmetic System timings are independent observations on the same prepared System AST; they are not paired arithmetic candidate ratios. “Invoke” for capture factories includes activation/first child use in the short arithmetic runner; sustained audit phases separate those operations.

Environment: modern .NET8.0.28/.NET10.0.9 ARM64; Framework4.8.9325.0 X64 on ARM64 Windows. AnyCPU runner headers alone do not establish process architecture. Ratios above1 mean slower; paired medians and ratios of ns medians are shown separately, with no weighted application-speedup claim.

Historical isolated cold costs are mixed; they are not measurements of final70f after its supported-host change. Decimal64 Standard modern saves6880B in every fresh pair; Framework saves14048B in every pair. Light modern retains12336B allocation-mode variation, including a positive sampled .NET10 capture-control median. Framework Light decimal64 cold paired time ratio1.265 is retained. No universal cold parity claim is made. Full minima/maxima, phases and individual deltas are in ../atomic-right-arithmetic/cold-analysis.json.

Exact source hashes, raw files, preserved measured Framework warm source, later cold CLI, native proof and an explicit reproduction script are in arithmetic reproduction manifest / ../atomic-right-arithmetic/REPRODUCTION.txt. Reproduction defaults to verification, preserves original observations and requires explicit new output plus build/measurement flags.

Frozen runtime, A/A and native audit at42f

The sustained audit uses11 samples rotating all six version orders, shared calibrated counts targeting40ms for the fastest delegate,4950 raw rows and90 correctness checks.25 samples below20ms remain flagged, not filtered. The separate A/A audit has1452 samples/66 phase rows and zero below20ms. It measures independent-target/context variation; its ratios are never subtracted from versioned measurements.

For the tested ordinary scalar trees, canonical finalized IL/signatures/locals/EH match prePR. TryCatch includes the required catch-entry Pop; correct EH hosting can differ even where emitted body bytes match. Readonly capture child/curry bodies match, while outer factories remove a shared-wrapper write. Mutable-value capture changes storage and removes24B per child call; mutable-reference Length uses Callvirt for correct receiver behavior. Framework unresolved internal local-signature handles remain UNKNOWN. Exact helper-source equality is not a proof of arbitrary callee or timed native placement equality. See ../../pr558-round3/runtime-parity/REPORT.md, ../../pr558-round3/runtime-parity/source-audit.json and ../../pr558-round3/runtime-aa/REPORT.md.

Supported EH hosting correction and map-capacity repair

Supported host commit: 70f297cbd5b2f232fd16ec0753b52f5c9ef387a0. Historical42f and isolated candidates used an anonymous-module constructor workaround on modern runtimes. It worked in tested release builds, but official .NET10 source explicitly forbids callers supplying that module; it is not a supported contract. The selected final source uses the public restricted anonymous constructor for exception-aware methods and ordinary owner hosting elsewhere. This legitimately moves JIT work into CreateDelegate, so compile-only is not comparable deferred-JIT work; compile+first, invocation and managed bytes remain separate.

Official evidence: DynamicMethod.Init anonymous-module assertion; restricted CreateDelegate eager compilation. Published acknowledgement: reply4319059045 to dadhi. Original request/reply JSON and focused proof are in the review-and-supported-host partition.

All comparisons/errors/notes remain in ../empty-finally/analysis.json.

New diagnostics remain separately classified: supplied-ref operator methods are factory-valid; prePR ignored the supplied method and produced wrong observable effects, while42f/arithmetic candidate honor it but emit invalid arguments. This boundary is neither “fully preexisting” nor repaired by arithmetic. Working byref-input/byvalue-operator forms match System. Ref-return Try-value controls have their own prePR/current/fixed/System logs; do not conflate them with supplied-ref operators.

The pending-arithmetic/outward-goto reproducer predates this PR: System returns17 while all16 compared FEC version/flavor/runtime combinations throw InvalidProgramException. It remains documented outside the original imported denominator. The deep-tree crash remains deferred by user instruction, with no broad refactor. No new passing test asserts invalid behavior and no original test was silently excluded.

Selected combined snapshot 70f297cbd5b2f232fd16ec0753b52f5c9ef387a0

This incremental comparison pins frozen42f vs the selected final snapshot plus System; its before baseline is not prePR. Arithmetic-isolated44ee and frozen e4/42f audit results remain historical records with their original sources. Exact source manifest: ../combined-final-bounded/manifest.json. Old6d preparation remains immutable and unmeasured: its full native run was interrupted by an unchanged-prePR SmallMap promotion-capacity bug, not a Try-value control-flow regression.

Final combined matrix is complete:12474 raw rows,198 phase groups and zero errors.109 samples under20ms and3027 under30ms remain retained. Compile-only uses independent30ms calibration per compiler; other FEC phases share counts, while System has its own calibration. No fresh-process final70f cold matrix was run; earlier cold allocation modes describe historical sources and unresolved risk, not measured final70f cold regressions.

Primary managed-byte marginal medians:28 increased,139 unchanged,31 decreased phase groups.22 positive differences are fractional (<0.324B/operation), with integer batch allocation events on both versions and differing compile counts; the exact allocators are not established. Six Framework Light capture1/capture8/mutable compile and compile+first groups retain approximately192B/operation. A separate equal-count allocation-only confirmation is preserved independently; it never replaces these primary observations.

Selected .NET10 Standard results show why phase separation matters: add compilation is about12.4× faster than System, while add invocation is1.072× System. Capture1 factory activation is0.097× System, but its preactivated child call is2.002× System; mutable child call is2.237× System and capture8 child call0.709× System. DecimalEH64 compile moves57.3× versus historical42f with its unsupported deferred host, while compile+first improves to0.703× historical42f and remains0.991× System. These are per-shape observations, not a general compiler ranking.
